### restore

`machines.restore`

Put a sandbox's disks back to a snapshot's, in place

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.restore({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |
| `idempotency_key` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `acceptMinimumLease` | `boolean` | No | As in StartMachineRequest: required when the restore boots a macOS sandbox whose Mac lease ended. |
| `desiredState` | `running \| stopped` | No | `running` (default) boots the restored disks; `stopped` leaves the sandbox stopped with them. |
| `snapshotId` | `string` | No | The snapshot whose disks the sandbox gets (`sn_...`, or an older `im_...` ID). Absent: the snapshot the sandbox was created from; a sandbox created from a template needs it (`400`). |
| `timeoutSeconds` | `number` | No | For a running sandbox: how long to wait for a clean shutdown before it is forced off (0-600; 0: the server default), as for stop. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `error` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `forkInherited` | `boolean \| null` | No | On the answer to `POST /v1/machines/{id}/fork`: the control host that took it keeps the source's delete deadline, idle auto-suspend, max run and metadata, and takes `autoSuspendAfterSeconds` and `maxRunMinutes`. Absent: a host from before that (during a rolling control deploy), which gave the fork its defaults with only the request's `metadata` and `deleteAfterSeconds`; set the idle auto-suspend and max run with `PATCH /v1/machines/{id}`. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `machine` | `object \| null` | No | The sandbox as it is when `GET /v1/operations/{id}` is read. Included when the operation `succeeded`, the sandbox still exists, and the caller may read sandboxes (`machines:read` for API keys). Later operations can change it; it is not a record of the state at completion. Absent otherwise, and from older servers. |
| `machineId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `phase` | `string \| null` | No | Free text progress, e.g. scheduling, booting, waiting_ready, snapshotting. |
| `scheduledAt` | `string \| null` | No | The stop or delete of a macOS sandbox inside its Mac lease's minimum is not done now but at this time (the sandbox's `archiveAt` or `deleteAt`); the operation is `succeeded`. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | `succeeded` and `failed` are terminal. |
| `trigger` | `string \| null` | No | Set when instabox started the operation itself: `idle` (paused when idle), `max_run` (maximum continuous runtime), `memory_pressure` (paused, its processes kept, because the host it ran on was running out of memory; the next use wakes it, as after `idle`), `request` (wake on use), `wake` (POST /v1/machines/&#123;id&#125;/wake), `timeout`, `archive`, `delete_at` (the sandbox's `deleteAt` passed), `drain` (maintenance by an admin), `user_delete` (an admin deleted the owner), `user_disable` (an admin disabled the owner), `admin` (an admin stopped or paused the sandbox), `admin_delete` (an admin deleted the sandbox), `credit` (its free-tier org used up the month's included usage), `spend_cap`, `downgrade`, `subscription_ended`, `payment_method` (its free workspace has no card on file), `members` (its free workspace has more members than the free plan allows), `early_allowance_ended` (paused because the account's early free-sandbox allowance ended and it has more free sandboxes than the free plan runs), `abuse_pool` (paused because the sandbox tried to connect to a blocked destination on the egress blocklist, such as a crypto-mining pool; a DNS lookup of a listed domain never suspends) or `abuse_cpu` (paused by the CPU abuse detector: a restricted account's sandbox ran flat out for an hour with no memory use, no public port, nobody using it and a connection to a mining port) or `abuse_scan` (paused by the flow detector: a restricted account's sandbox swept one port across many hosts, scanned many ports of one host or brute-forced a login port). Absent: a client request. |
| `twin` | `object \| null` | No | On the answer to a half-size macOS create that opened a Mac: the second sandbox the create made on it (a Mac runs two sandboxes of half its size). |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="error full type">

```ts
{
  at?: string | null,
  code?: string | null,
  docs?: string | null,
  docs_url?: string | null,
  hint?: string | null,
  message?: string | null,
  operationId?: string | null | null,
  reason?: string | null,
  requestId?: string | null,
  requiredScopes?: string[] | null,
  retryAfterSeconds?: number | null,
  retryable?: boolean | null
} | null | null
```

### resume

`machines.resume`

Resume a paused sandbox where it left off

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.resume({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |
| `idempotency_key` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `vault` | `boolean` | No | False disables legacy Vault delivery without changing org Secret selections. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `error` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `forkInherited` | `boolean \| null` | No | On the answer to `POST /v1/machines/{id}/fork`: the control host that took it keeps the source's delete deadline, idle auto-suspend, max run and metadata, and takes `autoSuspendAfterSeconds` and `maxRunMinutes`. Absent: a host from before that (during a rolling control deploy), which gave the fork its defaults with only the request's `metadata` and `deleteAfterSeconds`; set the idle auto-suspend and max run with `PATCH /v1/machines/{id}`. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `machine` | `object \| null` | No | The sandbox as it is when `GET /v1/operations/{id}` is read. Included when the operation `succeeded`, the sandbox still exists, and the caller may read sandboxes (`machines:read` for API keys). Later operations can change it; it is not a record of the state at completion. Absent otherwise, and from older servers. |
| `machineId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `phase` | `string \| null` | No | Free text progress, e.g. scheduling, booting, waiting_ready, snapshotting. |
| `scheduledAt` | `string \| null` | No | The stop or delete of a macOS sandbox inside its Mac lease's minimum is not done now but at this time (the sandbox's `archiveAt` or `deleteAt`); the operation is `succeeded`. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | `succeeded` and `failed` are terminal. |
| `trigger` | `string \| null` | No | Set when instabox started the operation itself: `idle` (paused when idle), `max_run` (maximum continuous runtime), `memory_pressure` (paused, its processes kept, because the host it ran on was running out of memory; the next use wakes it, as after `idle`), `request` (wake on use), `wake` (POST /v1/machines/&#123;id&#125;/wake), `timeout`, `archive`, `delete_at` (the sandbox's `deleteAt` passed), `drain` (maintenance by an admin), `user_delete` (an admin deleted the owner), `user_disable` (an admin disabled the owner), `admin` (an admin stopped or paused the sandbox), `admin_delete` (an admin deleted the sandbox), `credit` (its free-tier org used up the month's included usage), `spend_cap`, `downgrade`, `subscription_ended`, `payment_method` (its free workspace has no card on file), `members` (its free workspace has more members than the free plan allows), `early_allowance_ended` (paused because the account's early free-sandbox allowance ended and it has more free sandboxes than the free plan runs), `abuse_pool` (paused because the sandbox tried to connect to a blocked destination on the egress blocklist, such as a crypto-mining pool; a DNS lookup of a listed domain never suspends) or `abuse_cpu` (paused by the CPU abuse detector: a restricted account's sandbox ran flat out for an hour with no memory use, no public port, nobody using it and a connection to a mining port) or `abuse_scan` (paused by the flow detector: a restricted account's sandbox swept one port across many hosts, scanned many ports of one host or brute-forced a login port). Absent: a client request. |
| `twin` | `object \| null` | No | On the answer to a half-size macOS create that opened a Mac: the second sandbox the create made on it (a Mac runs two sandboxes of half its size). |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### secrets.get

`machines.secrets.get`

Read a sandbox's Secret selections and delivery status

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.secrets.get({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters) or the sandbox's name (unique among your sandboxes; names never contain `_`, so the two never collide). Older servers take only the ID and answer `404` for a name. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `directSecrets` | `object[] \| null` | No | Direct references (names and paths, never values), envSecrets included. |
| `envSecrets` | `string[] \| null` | No | Direct references that are exactly the same-name shorthand (&#123;secret X, env X&#125;, no file). Renamed and file references appear only in directSecrets. |
| `guest` | `object \| null` | No | Managed environment and public CA installation. This does not prove existing processes reloaded values. Removing env access cannot remove values already copied into process memory, files, or snapshots. |
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | Effective selected Secrets, including proxy all. Metadata only. |
| `proxy` | `object \| null` | No | Last node acknowledgement, not continuous health. Policies expire within 60 seconds unless renewed. Revocation is pending until applied or the previous lease expires. Already admitted streams may finish. |
| `secrets` | `string \| string[] \| null` | No | Proxy selection only. All includes current and future proxy Secrets in the sandbox's org. Explicit names resolve to stable IDs. An empty array selects none. Deleting and recreating a name does not restore an explicit grant. Null is rejected. |
| `usage` | `object[] \| null` | No | Present when the server records proxied request counts: one entry per selected proxy Secret. |
| `version` | `number \| null` | No | Version of this sandbox's selection configuration. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="directSecrets full type">

```ts
{
  env?: string | null,
  file?: string | null,
  secret?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="guest full type">

```ts
    id?: string | null,
    name?: string | null,
    version?: number | null
  }[] | null,

} | null
```

### ssh.createToken

`machines.ssh.createToken`

Issue an SSH token, for a plain `ssh` command without a key

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.ssh.createToken({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |
| `name` | `string` | No | Optional display name; whitespace is trimmed, at most 100 UTF-8 bytes. Existing callers may omit it. |
| `noExpiry` | `boolean` | No | A token that works until it is revoked: the reply has no `expiresAt`. Does not go with `ttlSeconds`. |
| `ttlSeconds` | `number` | No | Default 604800 (7 days), max 2592000 (30 days). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `command` | `string \| null` | No | `ssh -p <port> <token>@<host>` (without `-p` on port 22). |
| `expiresAt` | `string \| null` | No | Absent: the token does not expire (`noExpiry`). |
| `host` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `hostKeyFingerprint` | `string \| null` | No | SHA256 fingerprint of the SSH server's host key, which ssh asks to confirm on the first connection. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | Connection ID (`cn_…`) of the token's grant. |
| `machineId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `moshCommand` | `string \| null` | No | The same login with mosh: `mosh --ssh="ssh -p <port>" <token>@<host>` (`mosh <token>@<host>` on port 22). Absent when mosh is not available. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `port` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `token` | `string \| null` | No | `ibssh_` and 26 lowercase base32 characters (128 bits): the SSH user name. Shown only here. |

| `port` | `number \| null` | No | — |

### start

`machines.start`

Start a stopped or paused sandbox

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.start({
	id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd',
	queue: { autoLease: true },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |
| `idempotency_key` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `acceptMinimumLease` | `boolean` | No | As in CreateMachineRequest: required when the start of a macOS sandbox may open a Mac lease (its org's lease of its Mac ended); `400 minimum_lease_required` without it. |
| `host` | `string` | No | Admin only: place the sandbox on this host. While `storage.synced` is false the sandbox can only start on its current host; another host gets `409 operation_conflict`. |
| `memMiB` | `number` | No | New memory (MiB) of the stopped sandbox, as `vcpus`; absent: its current size. |
| `queue` | `object` | No | Wait in the GPU queue, or for a macOS sandbox in the Mac queue (`CreateMachineRequest.queue`, `StartMachineRequest.queue`); `{}` waits up to an hour for a GPU, up to 7 days for a Mac. |
| `rescue` | `boolean` | No | Boot a stopped sandbox without running its data-init.d hooks (`/usr/local/lib/instabox/data-init.d`, the template's and your own), so a hook that blocks the boot can be fixed from inside. Everything else of the boot runs; the next start runs the hooks again. A running sandbox is `409 operation_conflict` (stop it first), and so is a paused one, which resumes without booting (resume it, then stop it), and so is joining an active start that is not a rescue. A sandbox whose host runs a node-agent without rescue starts fails the operation (`host_unavailable`) and stays stopped. |
| `vault` | `boolean` | No | False disables legacy Vault delivery without changing org Secret selections. |
| `vcpus` | `number` | No | Start a stopped paid sandbox at another of the workspace's `allowedSpecs`, as `PATCH` `vcpus`/`memMiB` then `start` would: with `memMiB`, one of them (alone: the one size with that many vCPUs). Absent: its current size. The size is stored before the start and the boot asks for it; read it from the sandbox's `spec` (the start Operation carries no request body). Its current size changes nothing; a running sandbox at another size is `409 operation_conflict` (stop it first), and so is a paused one (resume it, then stop it), as is a sandbox whose start is already active (naming it: only the same size, or none, joins that start); another size, or the free sandbox, is `400 spec_not_allowed`; 0 or a negative is `400 bad_request`. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="queue full type">

```ts
{
  autoLease?: boolean,
  maxWaitSeconds?: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `error` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `forkInherited` | `boolean \| null` | No | On the answer to `POST /v1/machines/{id}/fork`: the control host that took it keeps the source's delete deadline, idle auto-suspend, max run and metadata, and takes `autoSuspendAfterSeconds` and `maxRunMinutes`. Absent: a host from before that (during a rolling control deploy), which gave the fork its defaults with only the request's `metadata` and `deleteAfterSeconds`; set the idle auto-suspend and max run with `PATCH /v1/machines/{id}`. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `machine` | `object \| null` | No | The sandbox as it is when `GET /v1/operations/{id}` is read. Included when the operation `succeeded`, the sandbox still exists, and the caller may read sandboxes (`machines:read` for API keys). Later operations can change it; it is not a record of the state at completion. Absent otherwise, and from older servers. |
| `machineId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `phase` | `string \| null` | No | Free text progress, e.g. scheduling, booting, waiting_ready, snapshotting. |
| `scheduledAt` | `string \| null` | No | The stop or delete of a macOS sandbox inside its Mac lease's minimum is not done now but at this time (the sandbox's `archiveAt` or `deleteAt`); the operation is `succeeded`. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | `succeeded` and `failed` are terminal. |
| `trigger` | `string \| null` | No | Set when instabox started the operation itself: `idle` (paused when idle), `max_run` (maximum continuous runtime), `memory_pressure` (paused, its processes kept, because the host it ran on was running out of memory; the next use wakes it, as after `idle`), `request` (wake on use), `wake` (POST /v1/machines/&#123;id&#125;/wake), `timeout`, `archive`, `delete_at` (the sandbox's `deleteAt` passed), `drain` (maintenance by an admin), `user_delete` (an admin deleted the owner), `user_disable` (an admin disabled the owner), `admin` (an admin stopped or paused the sandbox), `admin_delete` (an admin deleted the sandbox), `credit` (its free-tier org used up the month's included usage), `spend_cap`, `downgrade`, `subscription_ended`, `payment_method` (its free workspace has no card on file), `members` (its free workspace has more members than the free plan allows), `early_allowance_ended` (paused because the account's early free-sandbox allowance ended and it has more free sandboxes than the free plan runs), `abuse_pool` (paused because the sandbox tried to connect to a blocked destination on the egress blocklist, such as a crypto-mining pool; a DNS lookup of a listed domain never suspends) or `abuse_cpu` (paused by the CPU abuse detector: a restricted account's sandbox ran flat out for an hour with no memory use, no public port, nobody using it and a connection to a mining port) or `abuse_scan` (paused by the flow detector: a restricted account's sandbox swept one port across many hosts, scanned many ports of one host or brute-forced a login port). Absent: a client request. |
| `twin` | `object \| null` | No | On the answer to a half-size macOS create that opened a Mac: the second sandbox the create made on it (a Mac runs two sandboxes of half its size). |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### stop

`machines.stop`

Stop a running sandbox (shuts it down; running programs end, files are kept)

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

### update

`machines.update`

Rename, resize (vCPUs, memory, disk) a stopped sandbox, or set its idle pause and metadata

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.update({
	id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd',
	networkPolicy: { outbound: 'restricted' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |
| `idempotency_key` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `apply` | `restart` | No | `restart`: a running or paused sandbox whose change needs a stop is stopped, resized and started again by one `resize` operation (`202`); see the operation. Absent: such a change is `409`. Anything else is `400 bad_request`. |
| `autoSuspendAfterSeconds` | `number` | No | The plan's rule (`AutoSuspendAfterSeconds`): a paid sandbox takes 300-1800 in whole minutes, as sent, or 0 (off) where the plan allows always on; below 300, above 1800 or not a multiple of 60 is `400 bad_request`; the free sandbox takes only 1800 (else `403 spec_not_allowed`), and 0 without always on is `403 spec_not_allowed` too. A refused value leaves the setting unchanged; the reply shows the effective value. API keys follow the owner's policy. Separately, with manual lifecycle (`GET /v1/me`) or an admin sign-in session: 120-86400, or 0 (off). Absent: unchanged. |
| `cancelScheduled` | `boolean` | No | `true` clears a stop or delete scheduled for the end of a macOS sandbox's Mac lease minimum (`archiveAt` and `deleteAt`). |
| `maxRunMinutes` | `number` | No | Maximum continuous runtime in minutes. -1 is unlimited; 0 sleeps immediately. Independent of idle auto-pause; whichever condition is reached first pauses the sandbox. On a free machine the service sets it and only an admin session can change it: each run starts at 120 minutes and grows with use up to 480 (commands, file access, port requests, terminal or SSH traffic and CPU load count as use; activity leases and wake calls do not): https://instabox.sh/docs/machines/auto-pause#runtime-on-the-free-plan. A free machine that is not running reports 480, the most a run can last. The response reports the current limit from runStartedAt; it can change during the run. Paid machines default to -1 and their users can change this setting. Max-run suspension starts a cooldown, returning 409 machine_cooldown with Retry-After on every wake path, except on Pro and higher plans, which have none. By default it is 5 to 20 minutes (5 plus a random 0 to 15) for the first such pause in a row on the free plan. Back-to-back pauses on the free plan rest longer (60 to 75, then 120 to 135 minutes): "Longer cooldowns on the free plan" at https://instabox.sh/docs/machines/auto-pause#longer-cooldowns-on-the-free-plan. Once cooldown ends, 0 still refuses wake with 409 max_run_disabled until changed. |
| `memMiB` | `number` | No | New memory (MiB) of a stopped paid sandbox, as `vcpus`; 0 or absent: unchanged. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Replaces the metadata (same limits as `Metadata`); &#123;&#125; clears it; null or absent: unchanged. |
| `name` | `string` | No | New name, unique among the org's live sandboxes; 1-63 of a-z, 0-9, '-', starting with a letter or digit. Absent: unchanged. |
| `networkPolicy` | `object` | No | A sandbox's outbound network policy: where it may connect to on the internet. It only narrows: a destination the platform blocks stays blocked. Inbound access (SSH, exec, published ports) is not affected. A fork starts with its source's policy. Domains are allowed through DNS: the sandbox's resolver answers only the listed names and opens the firewall for the addresses it returns, for as long as the DNS record lives, so a program must use the system resolver, and a connection to an address that no allowed name resolved to is refused unless a range lists it. It is not strict data-loss prevention: a site that shares an address with an allowed one (a CDN) is reachable too. |
| `persistentDiskGiB` | `number` | No | New disk (`/data`) size in GiB, 1-1024, up to the plan's `maxDiskGiB`; 0 or absent: unchanged. Grow: a running box grows online (no restart, processes keep running); if it can't (rare: the box is unreachable or its host is older), `409 grow_needs_stop` (stop it and send the size again); a stopped box grows at its next start. Shrink: only while stopped (else `409 operation_conflict`), and only when the used data plus 10% (at least 1 GiB) fits; else `409 disk_too_small`, with the smallest size that fits in the message, and the box unchanged. Past 1024 is `400 bad_request`, and so is growing on a template without the `root-grow` capability (a shrink needs none); a free box's disk is fixed (`400 spec_not_allowed`, running or stopped). The Python SDK's `machines.resize(m, disk_gib)` sends this field. |
| `rootDiskGiB` | `number` | No | Admins only: new root filesystem size in GiB (the template sizes it). From anyone else it is `400 spec_not_allowed`; grow the disk with `persistentDiskGiB`. |
| `vault` | `boolean` | No | False disables legacy Vault delivery; true cannot re-enable the legacy flag; absent is unchanged. Org Secret selections are independent. |
| `vcpus` | `number` | No | New vCPUs of a stopped paid sandbox: with `memMiB`, one of the workspace's `allowedSpecs`, or of its template's `defaultSpec` and `sizes` when the template has a `pool` (alone: the one size with that many vCPUs); 0 or absent: unchanged. Applied at its next start (see the operation). Running is `409 operation_conflict` (stop it first), and so is paused (resume it, then stop it); another size, or the free sandbox, is `400 spec_not_allowed`. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="networkPolicy full type">

```ts
{
  allowCidrs?: string[],
  allowDomains?: string[],
  outbound: restricted | open
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### usage

`machines.usage`

What the sandbox used over a window, for billing

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.usage({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |
| `from` | `string` | No | — |
| `to` | `string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `diskGiBSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | Disk (`/data`) size, current size, times the seconds the sandbox existed in the window. |
| `egressBytes` | `number \| null` | No | Bytes the sandbox sent to the public internet, by whole hour: every hour from the one `from` falls in up to `to`. Billed or not (the plan's free egress and free sandboxes included). |
| `from` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `memGiBSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | Memory GiB times running seconds. |
| `runningSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | Seconds the sandbox was running in the window. |
| `to` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `vcpuSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | vCPUs times running seconds. |

### vault.get

`machines.vault.get`

What the Vault writes into this machine (docs/vault.md)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.vault.get({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters) or the sandbox's name (unique among your sandboxes; names never contain `_`, so the two never collide). Older servers take only the ID and answer `404` for a name. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `applied` | `object \| null` | No | The last write into the machine; null: none yet. |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | The machine takes the Vault. |
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The items that apply with the machine's current metadata (none when enabled is false). |

```ts
{
  at?: string | null,
  env?: string[] | null,
  error?: string | null,
  files?: {
    path?: string | null,
    sha256?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  kept?: {
    path?: string | null,
    reason?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  removed?: string[] | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>

```ts
{

  name?: string | null,
  path?: string | null
}[] | null
```

### vault.write

`machines.vault.write`

Write the current Vault items into the running machine now

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.vault.write({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters) or the sandbox's name (unique among your sandboxes; names never contain `_`, so the two never collide). Older servers take only the ID and answer `404` for a name. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `env` | `string[] \| null` | No | The variables exported. |
| `error` | `string \| null` | No | Why the write failed, when it did. |
| `files` | `object[] \| null` | No | The files written (or, when the write failed, those of the last write, kept for the next reconciliation). |
| `kept` | `object[] \| null` | No | Files that no longer apply but were left (changed in the machine since, or not writable). |
| `removed` | `string[] \| null` | No | Files of an earlier write removed because they no longer apply. |

```ts
  sha256?: string | null
}[] | null
```

```ts
{
  path?: string | null,
  reason?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### wake

`machines.wake`

Resume an idle-suspended machine before using it

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.machines.wake({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID (`m_` + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `abuseLock` | `object \| null` | No | The owner's account of the abuse check's pause behind a sandbox's cooldownReason: when, what the sandbox did in a sentence (explanation) and in detail (what, with the exact address of a blocked destination, the address and port of a brute force, the swept port or scanned host and the count of a scan, the CPU share and, when the check confirmed it, the mining program and its pool), and how to appeal. |
| `archiveAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the sandbox is stopped if it is running (set at creation with `archiveSeconds`): its files are kept and a start brings it back. Resuming does not clear it; a sandbox that is not running when it passes only has it cleared. Absent: none. |
| `autoSuspendAfterSeconds` | `number \| null \| null` | No | The running sandbox is paused once it has been idle this many seconds (no agent working, no terminal input or output, little CPU); null: off. Pause, resume, stop and start keep it. |
| `autoSuspendedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the sandbox was queued to pause automatically; a successful automatic resume or its next explicit operation clears it. Absent: none. |
| `cooldownReason` | `string \| null` | No | While cooldownUntil has not passed, the automatic abuse check whose pause locks the sandbox, the same as a wake's 409 `machine_cooldown` Error.reason: `abuse_egress_blocklist` (it tried to connect to a blocked destination on the egress blocklist, not a DNS lookup), `abuse_network_flows` (scanning or attack traffic), `abuse_cpu` (mining). Absent for a max-run cooldown, and once the lock ends. |
| `cooldownUntil` | `string \| null \| null` | No | All wake paths are refused until this deadline, including admin actions: after a max-run suspension, or the 24-hour lock of an automatic abuse check's pause (cooldownReason). |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdByApp` | `object \| null` | No | The public part of an OAuth app, shown on consent pages, grants and sandboxes. |
| `deleteAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the sandbox is deleted, whatever its state (set at creation with `deleteAfterSeconds`). The delete is an ordinary `delete` operation (`trigger: delete_at`), queued at `deleteAt` (within about a second) once no other operation is in progress. Absent: never. |
| `desiredState` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `earlyAllowanceEnded` | `boolean \| null` | No | Set on a stopped or paused free sandbox that cannot be started because its org's early free-sandbox allowance ended and as many free sandboxes as the standard limit allows are running: a start is refused (`402 early_allowance_ended`) until one is paused or deleted. Absent otherwise. |
| `envNames` | `string[] \| null` | No | The names of the sandbox's create-time `env`, sorted. Absent when it has none. The values are never shown: read them in the sandbox. |
| `free` | `boolean \| null` | No | A free-tier org's sandbox (docs/pricing.md): never billed, pauses after 30 idle minutes (another `autoSuspendAfterSeconds` is refused with 403). On a paid plan every sandbox is billed. |
| `guestUser` | `string \| null` | No | The Linux user in the sandbox that SSH sessions, exec and the terminal log in as, fixed at create: the `guestUser` the create named, else `user` (a create from a snapshot: the snapshot's user). `dev` on sandboxes from templates without the `guest-user` capability, and on every sandbox created before the field. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `image` | `object \| null` | No | Deprecated: `snapshot`, under its old name (same value). |
| `lastError` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `lastOom` | `object \| null` | No | A process the sandbox's kernel OOM killer killed. |
| `lease` | `object \| null` | No | The Mac lease a macOS sandbox runs under. Absent on every other sandbox, and while a macOS sandbox runs under none (stopped after its lease ended). |
| `maxRunMinutes` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum continuous runtime in minutes. -1 is unlimited; 0 sleeps immediately. Independent of idle auto-pause; whichever condition is reached first pauses the sandbox. On a free machine the service sets it and only an admin session can change it: each run starts at 120 minutes and grows with use up to 480 (commands, file access, port requests, terminal or SSH traffic and CPU load count as use; activity leases and wake calls do not): https://instabox.sh/docs/machines/auto-pause#runtime-on-the-free-plan. A free machine that is not running reports 480, the most a run can last. The response reports the current limit from runStartedAt; it can change during the run. Paid machines default to -1 and their users can change this setting. Max-run suspension starts a cooldown, returning 409 machine_cooldown with Retry-After on every wake path, except on Pro and higher plans, which have none. By default it is 5 to 20 minutes (5 plus a random 0 to 15) for the first such pause in a row on the free plan. Back-to-back pauses on the free plan rest longer (60 to 75, then 120 to 135 minutes): "Longer cooldowns on the free plan" at https://instabox.sh/docs/machines/auto-pause#longer-cooldowns-on-the-free-plan. Once cooldown ends, 0 still refuses wake with 409 max_run_disabled until changed. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | String labels. At most 32 keys of 1-64 characters `[A-Za-z0-9_.-]`, values of at most 512 characters, 8 KiB as JSON. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `networkPolicy` | `object \| null` | No | A sandbox's outbound network policy: where it may connect to on the internet. It only narrows: a destination the platform blocks stays blocked. Inbound access (SSH, exec, published ports) is not affected. A fork starts with its source's policy. Domains are allowed through DNS: the sandbox's resolver answers only the listed names and opens the firewall for the addresses it returns, for as long as the DNS record lives, so a program must use the system resolver, and a connection to an address that no allowed name resolved to is refused unless a range lists it. It is not strict data-loss prevention: a site that shares an address with an allowed one (a CDN) is reachable too. |
| `oomKills` | `number \| null` | No | Processes the sandbox's kernel OOM killer killed since the sandbox was created (out of memory, or a memory cgroup's limit). Read from the sandbox about once a minute while it runs, and after an exec that exits non-zero; 0 on sandboxes whose host or guest agent predates the reading (absent from older servers). |
| `operationId` | `string \| null \| null` | No | The active operation, if any. |
| `orgId` | `string \| null` | No | The org the sandbox counts against (docs/orgs.md). |
| `queue` | `object \| null` | No | A sandbox's live GPU queue or Mac queue entry (`Machine.queue`). A GPU entry has `gpuType` and `gpus`; a Mac entry has `pool`. |
| `readyAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the running sandbox last became ready (`runtime.ready` turned true: SSH, exec and files work). For a new sandbox, `readyAt` minus `createdAt` is its create time; after a start or resume it is that start's. Absent while the sandbox is not ready, and on a sandbox that was already ready before the field existed (until its next start or resume). |
| `revision` | `number \| null` | No | Increases with each accepted change. |
| `runStartedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Start of the current continuous run, conservatively initialized on a first error observation too; resets only after confirmed sleep. |
| `runtime` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `snapshot` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `spec` | `object \| null` | No | A sandbox's size. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `storage` | `object \| null` | No | The sandbox's storage (`/data`, where HOME lives; `/` on macOS, whose disk is the root volume). |
| `suspendable` | `boolean \| null` | No | false: the sandbox cannot be paused, since its hosts keep no memory of a running sandbox (macOS). Stop the sandbox instead; its files are kept. Absent: it can. |
| `template` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `vault` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether legacy Vault delivery is enabled. Independent of org Secret selections and delivery status at the sandbox's /secrets endpoint. |

### get

`me.get`

The signed-in user and the sandbox size they get

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.me.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `activated` | `boolean \| null` | No | This account passed the invite gate (admins always have). When `inviteRequired` and not `activated`, every route but this one, `POST /v1/invites/redeem` and `POST /v1/auth/logout` answers `403 invite_required`. |
| `admin` | `boolean \| null` | No | The user is an admin and may use the admin-only endpoints (`/v1/admin`). |
| `adminUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Admins only: the origin of the admin console when it has its own (the server's `adminUrl`). Absent, the console is `/admin/` on the dashboard's origin. |
| `allowedSpecs` | `object[] \| null` | No | The sizes your new sandboxes may have in your personal org (no `rootDiskGiB`: the template's). The first is the server's `limits.machineSpec` (4 vCPUs and 8 GiB memory by default), what a create naming no size gets; the others are your plan's (Pro: 2/4, 4/8, 8/16; Builder: 2/4 up to 16/32; Team and Enterprise: 2/4 up to 32/64) and sizes an admin granted you, picked by naming their `vcpus` and `memMiB` on create. Any other size is refused with `spec_not_allowed`, admins included. |
| `alwaysOnAllowed` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether 0 (off) is a choice too, on `org`'s plan (the personal org's for a sign-in session). A sandbox in another org follows that org's plan: see its `workspaces` entry's `alwaysOnAllowed`. |
| `avatarUrl` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `billingEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Paid plans can be subscribed: the server has Stripe configured. False: the dashboard offers no plan actions and the billing routes answer 503. |
| `csrfToken` | `string \| null` | No | Browser sessions only. Send it as `X-Instabox-CSRF` on every request that is not GET or HEAD. |
| `directSecretRefs` | `boolean \| null` | No | This site takes direct Secret file references and renamed variables (`directSecrets`) and exec `secretEnvs`. Absent: they are refused with 400 (same-name references work); clients should not send them, since a control host from before them would ignore or misread them. |
| `disabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | An administrator disabled the account. Absent otherwise. |
| `diskResize` | `string \| null` | No | An admin's override of disk growth (`PATCH /v1/machines/{id}` `persistentDiskGiB`, `rootDiskGiB`): `allow` grows free sandboxes' disks too, `deny` none. Absent: paid sandboxes grow, free ones are fixed. |
| `email` | `string \| null` | No | The account's email, which an org invite must be sent to for it to join. |
| `emailInvites` | `boolean \| null` | No | This server emails workspace invites (server.json `email`). Absent: creating or resending an invite answers 503. |
| `exportOnly` | `boolean \| null` | No | This session can only export the account's own data (docs/data-export.md, "Disabled accounts"): `GET /v1/orgs`, `GET` and `POST /v1/orgs/{id}/exports`, `GET /v1/exports/{id}` and its download. Every other route answers `403 account_disabled`. Absent otherwise. |
| `fixedAutoSuspendSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | The idle auto-suspend every sandbox of this account gets on a server that offers no choice; absent when the account chooses (`manualLifecycle`, or `idleChoicesSeconds`). |
| `idleChoicesSeconds` | `number[] \| null` | No | The idle times offered (300 to 1800, 5 to 30 minutes; docs/pricing.md): a request may send any whole minute from the first to the last. 0 (off) is never listed: `alwaysOnAllowed` says whether it is a choice too. Absent with `manualLifecycle`. |
| `inviteRequired` | `boolean \| null` | No | This server gates new accounts behind an invite code (docs/invites.md). |
| `login` | `string \| null` | No | GitHub login at last sign-in (display only). |
| `macQueue` | `boolean \| null` | No | The server's Mac queue is on (`macQueue`): a macOS create or start that sends `queue` waits for a Mac when none is free. Absent: off, and `queue` on such a request is refused. |
| `machineMetrics` | `boolean \| null` | No | Clients show this account per-sandbox metrics (the dashboard's Metrics tab, `instabox metrics`): admins always, every account once the server sets `machineMetricsForAll`. Absent: hidden. `GET /v1/machines/{id}/metrics` answers either way. |
| `manualLifecycle` | `boolean \| null` | No | An admin let this account manage its sandboxes' lifecycle (docs/idle-suspend.md): the `autoSuspendAfterSeconds` it sends applies (0: off). Otherwise every sandbox pauses after `fixedAutoSuspendSeconds` idle, whatever a request says. |
| `movePersonal` | `boolean \| null` | No | This account may move its personal plan into a workspace it owns (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/billing/move-personal`): admins always, every account once the server sets `movePersonalForAll`. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `networkPolicy` | `boolean \| null` | No | The server offers outbound network policies to this caller: `networkPolicy` is a field of create, fork and `PATCH /v1/machines/{id}`, and clients show the feature. Absent: the field is unknown to the server (`400 unknown_field`). |
| `oauthPublishing` | `boolean \| null` | No | This account may create and edit OAuth apps (`/v1/orgs/{id}/oauth/apps`): admins always, every account once the server sets `oauth.openPublishing`. Absent: hidden; those routes answer `403`. |
| `org` | `object \| null` | No | The org `allowedSpecs`, `idleChoicesSeconds` and `alwaysOnAllowed` describe: your personal org, or a workspace key's workspace. Absent from older servers. |
| `orgId` | `string \| null` | No | Set for a workspace API key: its workspace. The user is then the workspace's owner, whom the key acts as. |
| `paidActivation` | `boolean \| null` | No | An account the invite gate holds back may buy a workspace (`POST /v1/orgs`) instead, and is activated once its plan takes effect (docs/invites.md). Absent: only an invite code lets it in. |
| `plans` | `object[] \| null` | No | The paid plans an org can go on (Pro, Builder, Team) as this server sells them, for plan pickers. |
| `resizeRestart` | `boolean \| null` | No | This server takes `PATCH /v1/machines/{id}` `apply: "restart"` (one stop, resize, start operation). Absent: it answers `400 resize_restart_off`; resize with your own stop, `PATCH`, start. |
| `termsVersion` | `string \| null` | No | The version of the rules of the road (the Terms of Service's acceptable use) this account last agreed to (`PUT /v1/me/terms`). Absent: none. |
| `userId` | `number \| null` | No | Instabox user ID. |
| `waitlist` | `object \| null` | No | The account's place in the line invite codes go out from and its referral link (docs/invites.md, "Referrals"). Absent: not in line (activated, the gate off, or a live code claimed for its email). |
| `webhooksEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | This server offers org webhooks (docs/webhooks.md). False: the dashboard hides them, the CLI says they are off and the webhook routes answer 404. |
| `workspaces` | `object[] \| null` | No | Your other orgs, each with its own plan and limits (`GET /v1/usage?org=ID`, `GET /v1/orgs/{id}`). Omitted when there are none, and for a workspace API key, which reaches only its own org. |

## Operations

### get

`operations.get`

Get an operation (the progress of a sandbox change)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.operations.get({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Operation ID (`op_…`). |
| `wait` | `string` | No | Long-poll for up to this long: a duration such as `25s` or `500ms`, or whole seconds (`25`). Capped at 30s; absent or `0` answers at once. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `error` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `forkInherited` | `boolean \| null` | No | On the answer to `POST /v1/machines/{id}/fork`: the control host that took it keeps the source's delete deadline, idle auto-suspend, max run and metadata, and takes `autoSuspendAfterSeconds` and `maxRunMinutes`. Absent: a host from before that (during a rolling control deploy), which gave the fork its defaults with only the request's `metadata` and `deleteAfterSeconds`; set the idle auto-suspend and max run with `PATCH /v1/machines/{id}`. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `machine` | `object \| null` | No | The sandbox as it is when `GET /v1/operations/{id}` is read. Included when the operation `succeeded`, the sandbox still exists, and the caller may read sandboxes (`machines:read` for API keys). Later operations can change it; it is not a record of the state at completion. Absent otherwise, and from older servers. |
| `machineId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `phase` | `string \| null` | No | Free text progress, e.g. scheduling, booting, waiting_ready, snapshotting. |
| `scheduledAt` | `string \| null` | No | The stop or delete of a macOS sandbox inside its Mac lease's minimum is not done now but at this time (the sandbox's `archiveAt` or `deleteAt`); the operation is `succeeded`. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | `succeeded` and `failed` are terminal. |
| `trigger` | `string \| null` | No | Set when instabox started the operation itself: `idle` (paused when idle), `max_run` (maximum continuous runtime), `memory_pressure` (paused, its processes kept, because the host it ran on was running out of memory; the next use wakes it, as after `idle`), `request` (wake on use), `wake` (POST /v1/machines/&#123;id&#125;/wake), `timeout`, `archive`, `delete_at` (the sandbox's `deleteAt` passed), `drain` (maintenance by an admin), `user_delete` (an admin deleted the owner), `user_disable` (an admin disabled the owner), `admin` (an admin stopped or paused the sandbox), `admin_delete` (an admin deleted the sandbox), `credit` (its free-tier org used up the month's included usage), `spend_cap`, `downgrade`, `subscription_ended`, `payment_method` (its free workspace has no card on file), `members` (its free workspace has more members than the free plan allows), `early_allowance_ended` (paused because the account's early free-sandbox allowance ended and it has more free sandboxes than the free plan runs), `abuse_pool` (paused because the sandbox tried to connect to a blocked destination on the egress blocklist, such as a crypto-mining pool; a DNS lookup of a listed domain never suspends) or `abuse_cpu` (paused by the CPU abuse detector: a restricted account's sandbox ran flat out for an hour with no memory use, no public port, nobody using it and a connection to a mining port) or `abuse_scan` (paused by the flow detector: a restricted account's sandbox swept one port across many hosts, scanned many ports of one host or brute-forced a login port). Absent: a client request. |
| `twin` | `object \| null` | No | On the answer to a half-size macOS create that opened a Mac: the second sandbox the create made on it (a Mac runs two sandboxes of half its size). |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Orgs

### billing.cancelSubscription

`orgs.billing.cancelSubscription`

End the org's subscription at the end of the current period. The owner; signed-in sessions only

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.billing.cancelSubscription({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Org ID (org_...). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `allowedSpecs` | `object[] \| null` | No | The sizes that may be created in the org, the default first (its plan's, then any an admin granted the org). |
| `billing` | `object \| null` | No | The org's Stripe subscription (docs/pricing.md); absent when it never had one. |
| `cardMissingAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while a workspace has no card on file. On the free tier its sandboxes then answer `402 payment_method_required` and running ones are paused (`trigger: payment_method`); they run again once one is added (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/billing/checkout` with no tier, or the portal). |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `disabledAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while the workspace cannot be used: its plan ended (cancelled, unpaid, or its credits ran out). Its sandboxes answer `402 subscription_required`; they and its snapshots are deleted 30 days later unless it subscribes again. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `kind` | `string \| null` | No | `personal`: the account's own org, made when it signed up, on any tier but with no other members: it takes no invites. `workspace`: an org to share, on any tier; on the free tier it needs a card on file and has up to `limits.maxMembers` members. |
| `limits` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `personal` | `boolean \| null` | No | `kind` is `personal`. |
| `purgeAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set on a disabled workspace: when its sandboxes and snapshots are deleted. Until then its people can download their `/data` (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/exports`). |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | Your role in the org. |
| `tier` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `usage` | `object \| null` | No | The org's usage this calendar month (UTC) as the hourly rating job priced it: running time by sandbox size, the allocated disk (`/data`) of the paid sandboxes beyond the plan's included disk, and egress beyond the plan's free egress a month, up to `ratedUntil`. Snapshots and the system disk are not billed. |

```ts
}[] | null
```

### billing.checkout

`orgs.billing.checkout`

Put an org on a paid plan, its credits first and a card for the rest. The owner; signed-in sessions only

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.billing.checkout({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Org ID (org_...). |
| `tier` | `pro \| builder \| team` | No | The tier to subscribe to; absent: a disabled workspace's own, or on a free workspace a card to save. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `org` | `object \| null` | No | An org (docs/orgs.md). Sandboxes are created in one and count against its plan. |
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | The Stripe Checkout URL to send the user to; empty when the org is on the plan already (its credits covered the rest of the month, or the card on file paid). |

### billing.movePersonal

`orgs.billing.movePersonal`

Move your personal plan, sandboxes and credits into a workspace you own (needs a signed-in user token; an API key gets 403)

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.billing.movePersonal({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Workspace ID (org_...) to move into. |
| `dryRun` | `boolean` | No | Report what would happen; change nothing. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `apiKeys` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `checkoutNeeded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The credits do not cover feeUsd: the workspace is bought in Checkout first, and nothing moves. |
| `checkoutUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Off a dry run with checkoutNeeded: the Checkout buying the workspace. |
| `created` | `boolean \| null` | No | Into a new workspace (`POST /v1/orgs/move-personal`): it was made; `into` is its ID, the ID the personal org had. |
| `creditId` | `string \| null` | No | The new grant holding the rest, expiring a year after the move (not set on a dry run). |
| `credits` | `string[] \| null` | No | Credit grant IDs moved, balance and expiry unchanged. |
| `dryRun` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `endedTier` | `string \| null` | No | The personal plan that ended; absent: none was active. |
| `expiredUsd` | `number \| null` | No | Of it, the share of grants that expired since the fee was drawn: not carried, since a move never extends a grant's life (estimated on a dry run too). |
| `feeUsd` | `number \| null` | No | The new workspace's first fee, drawn from the moved credits. |
| `from` | `string \| null` | No | The personal org. After a move into a new workspace: the caller's new, empty personal org (the one before is `into`). |
| `into` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `keptApiKeys` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `machines` | `string[] \| null` | No | Sandbox IDs moved. |
| `madePaid` | `string[] \| null` | No | Moved free sandboxes that became paid. |
| `movedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `movedFunding` | `string \| null` | No | What pays the moved plan. |
| `movedTier` | `string \| null` | No | The personal plan moved into the workspace with its subscription, card and renewal date (a workspace without a plan of its own); nothing was charged for it, and endedTier is absent. unusedUsd is then the rest of this month's allowance, moved into the workspace's month. |
| `overageUsd` | `number \| null` | No | With movedTier: the personal usage so far beyond this month's allowance, billed at the move (credits first, then the card). |
| `paused` | `string[] \| null` | No | Running sandboxes paused for the move and resumed after it. |
| `returnedUsd` | `number \| null` | No | Of it, what went back into the grants that paid the fee (estimated on a dry run). |
| `secrets` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `snapshots` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `tier` | `string \| null` | No | The new workspace's plan. |
| `unusedUsd` | `number \| null` | No | Unused share of this month's personal fee, carried as workspace credit. |

| `allowedSpecs` | `object[] \| null` | No | The sizes that may be created in the org, the default first (its plan's, then any an admin granted the org). |
| `billing` | `object \| null` | No | The org's Stripe subscription (docs/pricing.md); absent when it never had one. |
| `cardMissingAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while a workspace has no card on file. On the free tier its sandboxes then answer `402 payment_method_required` and running ones are paused (`trigger: payment_method`); they run again once one is added (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/billing/checkout` with no tier, or the portal). |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `disabledAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while the workspace cannot be used: its plan ended (cancelled, unpaid, or its credits ran out). Its sandboxes answer `402 subscription_required`; they and its snapshots are deleted 30 days later unless it subscribes again. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `kind` | `string \| null` | No | `personal`: the account's own org, made when it signed up, on any tier but with no other members: it takes no invites. `workspace`: an org to share, on any tier; on the free tier it needs a card on file and has up to `limits.maxMembers` members. |
| `limits` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `personal` | `boolean \| null` | No | `kind` is `personal`. |
| `purgeAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set on a disabled workspace: when its sandboxes and snapshots are deleted. Until then its people can download their `/data` (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/exports`). |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | Your role in the org. |
| `tier` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `usage` | `object \| null` | No | The org's usage this calendar month (UTC) as the hourly rating job priced it: running time by sandbox size, the allocated disk (`/data`) of the paid sandboxes beyond the plan's included disk, and egress beyond the plan's free egress a month, up to `ratedUntil`. Snapshots and the system disk are not billed. |

### billing.topup

`orgs.billing.topup`

Buy credits for an org on Pro, Builder or Team. The owner and admins; signed-in sessions only

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.billing.topup({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd', amountUSD: 25 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Org ID (org_...). |
| `idempotency_key` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `amountUSD` | `number` | Yes | The credits, whole USD, before tax. |
| `newCard` | `boolean` | No | Pay with another card in Stripe Checkout instead of the card on file. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `creditsUSD` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `topUp` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | The Stripe Checkout page (newCard). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="topUp full type">

```ts
{
  amountUSD?: number | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  creditId?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  method?: string | null,
  paidAt?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  taxUSD?: number | null,
  totalUSD?: number | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### billing.update

`orgs.billing.update`

Set a paid org's spend cap, usage alerts or extra starts. The owner and admins; signed-in sessions only

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.billing.update({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Org ID (org_...). |
| `extraStarts` | `pay \| fail` | No | What a create past the plan's included starts does: `fail` (refused, the default) or `pay` (charged, once an owner or admin turns it on). |
| `overageAlert` | `boolean` | No | Whether to email when usage goes beyond what the month includes. |
| `spendCapUSD` | `number` | No | The cap on this month's usage beyond the allowance; 0: nothing beyond it; -1: no cap. |
| `usageAlertPercent` | `number` | No | The usage alert's share of what the month includes; 0: off. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `allowedSpecs` | `object[] \| null` | No | The sizes that may be created in the org, the default first (its plan's, then any an admin granted the org). |
| `billing` | `object \| null` | No | The org's Stripe subscription (docs/pricing.md); absent when it never had one. |
| `cardMissingAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while a workspace has no card on file. On the free tier its sandboxes then answer `402 payment_method_required` and running ones are paused (`trigger: payment_method`); they run again once one is added (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/billing/checkout` with no tier, or the portal). |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `disabledAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while the workspace cannot be used: its plan ended (cancelled, unpaid, or its credits ran out). Its sandboxes answer `402 subscription_required`; they and its snapshots are deleted 30 days later unless it subscribes again. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `kind` | `string \| null` | No | `personal`: the account's own org, made when it signed up, on any tier but with no other members: it takes no invites. `workspace`: an org to share, on any tier; on the free tier it needs a card on file and has up to `limits.maxMembers` members. |
| `limits` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `personal` | `boolean \| null` | No | `kind` is `personal`. |
| `purgeAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set on a disabled workspace: when its sandboxes and snapshots are deleted. Until then its people can download their `/data` (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/exports`). |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | Your role in the org. |
| `tier` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `usage` | `object \| null` | No | The org's usage this calendar month (UTC) as the hourly rating job priced it: running time by sandbox size, the allocated disk (`/data`) of the paid sandboxes beyond the plan's included disk, and egress beyond the plan's free egress a month, up to `ratedUntil`. Snapshots and the system disk are not billed. |

### create

`orgs.create`

Buy a workspace through Stripe Checkout. Signed-in sessions only

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.create({ name: 'OPENAI_API_KEY', tier: 'pro' });
```

| `tier` | `pro \| builder \| team` | Yes | — |

| `org` | `object \| null` | No | An org (docs/orgs.md). Sandboxes are created in one and count against its plan. |
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | The Stripe Checkout URL to send the user to; empty when the org is on the plan already (its credits covered the rest of the month, or the card on file paid). |

| `snapshotId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### get

`orgs.get`

An org you belong to, with its limits and this month's usage

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.get({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Org ID (org_...). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `allowedSpecs` | `object[] \| null` | No | The sizes that may be created in the org, the default first (its plan's, then any an admin granted the org). |
| `billing` | `object \| null` | No | The org's Stripe subscription (docs/pricing.md); absent when it never had one. |
| `cardMissingAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while a workspace has no card on file. On the free tier its sandboxes then answer `402 payment_method_required` and running ones are paused (`trigger: payment_method`); they run again once one is added (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/billing/checkout` with no tier, or the portal). |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `disabledAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while the workspace cannot be used: its plan ended (cancelled, unpaid, or its credits ran out). Its sandboxes answer `402 subscription_required`; they and its snapshots are deleted 30 days later unless it subscribes again. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `kind` | `string \| null` | No | `personal`: the account's own org, made when it signed up, on any tier but with no other members: it takes no invites. `workspace`: an org to share, on any tier; on the free tier it needs a card on file and has up to `limits.maxMembers` members. |
| `limits` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `personal` | `boolean \| null` | No | `kind` is `personal`. |
| `purgeAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set on a disabled workspace: when its sandboxes and snapshots are deleted. Until then its people can download their `/data` (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/exports`). |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | Your role in the org. |
| `tier` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `usage` | `object \| null` | No | The org's usage this calendar month (UTC) as the hourly rating job priced it: running time by sandbox size, the allocated disk (`/data`) of the paid sandboxes beyond the plan's included disk, and egress beyond the plan's free egress a month, up to `ratedUntil`. Snapshots and the system disk are not billed. |

### github.jobs.list

`orgs.github.jobs.list`

The org's GitHub Actions jobs run in CI boxes, with why a job waits or did not start

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.github.jobs.list({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Org ID (org_...). |
| `state` | `all \| running \| waiting \| succeeded \| failed` | No | The jobs of one state group: `running` (`starting` or `started`), `waiting`, `succeeded` (`completed` with conclusion `success`) or `failed` (`completed` with conclusion `failure` or `timed_out`, `refused` or `failed`; `cancelled` and `skipped` are not failures). |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Page size. |
| `from` | `string` | No | Only the jobs queued at or after this time (RFC 3339); `counts` follow it. |
| `to` | `string` | No | Only the jobs queued before this time (RFC 3339), after `from`; `counts` follow it. |
| `offset` | `number` | No | Jobs of the group to skip. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `counts` | `object \| null` | No | The org's jobs in each state group that `state` takes. |
| `hasMore` | `boolean \| null` | No | More jobs of the state group asked for follow this page (next: offset + limit). |
| `installUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Where an owner or admin installs the App and links it to the org, in a browser signed in to instabox. |
| `jobs` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `linked` | `boolean \| null` | No | An install of the GitHub App is linked to the org. |
| `setup` | `object \| null` | No | What the CI page's setup steps count: only the jobs of the installs linked to the org now. An App uninstalled and installed again is a new install, so the setup starts over while the old jobs stay listed and in `counts`. |
| `unlinked` | `object[] \| null` | No | Installs unlinked from the org when the member who linked them left it; their jobs start nothing until an owner or admin links them again. |

```ts
  succeeded?: number | null,

} | null
```

### github.stats

`orgs.github.stats`

The org's GitHub Actions jobs of the last 7 days, today included, or of a time range, for the CI page

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.github.stats({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Org ID (org_...). |
| `tz` | `string` | No | The IANA time zone days are counted in, such as America/Los_Angeles. |
| `from` | `string` | No | Only jobs queued at or after this time (RFC 3339); needs step. |
| `to` | `string` | No | Only jobs queued before this time (RFC 3339); after from, and needs step. |
| `step` | `number` | No | Count the jobs in buckets of this many seconds from from (needed) to to or now, at most 1000 buckets; perDay is then empty. A day (86400) counts the days of tz from from's midnight, each a day also when the clocks change. Needed with from or to: without it the days are the last 7. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `buckets` | `object[] \| null` | No | With step: every step from from, oldest first. |
| `bySize` | `object[] \| null` | No | Each size with jobs, small to large. |
| `days` | `number \| null` | No | How many days, today included. |
| `jobs` | `number \| null` | No | Jobs queued. |
| `passed` | `number \| null` | No | Of those, jobs with conclusion `success`. |
| `perDay` | `object[] \| null` | No | Every day, oldest first. |
| `ran` | `number \| null` | No | Jobs that ran to their end on a CI box, whatever their conclusion. |
| `run` | `object \| null` | No | Seconds from a runner taking the job to its end. Absent when no job ended. |
| `step` | `number \| null` | No | The step asked for, in seconds. |
| `wait` | `object \| null` | No | Seconds from queued to a runner taking the job. Absent when no job was taken. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="buckets full type">

```ts
{
  failed?: number | null,
  other?: number | null,
  passed?: number | null,
  start?: string | null
}[] | null
```

<Accordion title="bySize full type">

```ts
{
  jobs?: number | null,
  p50?: number | null,
  p90?: number | null,
  size?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

```ts
  failed?: number | null,

  passed?: number | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

```ts
} | null
```

### keys.create

`orgs.keys.create`

Create an API key of an org. The owner and admins (a personal org's owner); signed-in sessions only

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.keys.create({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd', name: 'OPENAI_API_KEY' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Org ID (org_...). |
| `expiresInDays` | `number` | No | Days until the key expires, 1-365. 0 or absent: it does not expire. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `scopes` | `string[]` | No | `APIKeyScope`s, or in a workspace `workspace:admin` (also what absent means there). A personal org's key needs some. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `apiKey` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `key` | `string \| null` | No | The secret, shown only in this reply and to polls repeated within the 10-minute delivery window. |

```ts
} | null
```

### movePersonal

`orgs.movePersonal`

Move your personal plan, sandboxes and credits into a new workspace (needs a signed-in user token; an API key gets 403)

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.movePersonal({ name: 'OPENAI_API_KEY' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `dryRun` | `boolean` | No | Report what would happen; change nothing. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The new workspace's name. |
| `tier` | `pro \| builder \| team` | No | Optional: the personal plan's tier, which the workspace keeps. Another tier is refused. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `apiKeys` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `checkoutNeeded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The credits do not cover feeUsd: the workspace is bought in Checkout first, and nothing moves. |
| `checkoutUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Off a dry run with checkoutNeeded: the Checkout buying the workspace. |
| `created` | `boolean \| null` | No | Into a new workspace (`POST /v1/orgs/move-personal`): it was made; `into` is its ID, the ID the personal org had. |
| `creditId` | `string \| null` | No | The new grant holding the rest, expiring a year after the move (not set on a dry run). |
| `credits` | `string[] \| null` | No | Credit grant IDs moved, balance and expiry unchanged. |
| `dryRun` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `endedTier` | `string \| null` | No | The personal plan that ended; absent: none was active. |
| `expiredUsd` | `number \| null` | No | Of it, the share of grants that expired since the fee was drawn: not carried, since a move never extends a grant's life (estimated on a dry run too). |
| `feeUsd` | `number \| null` | No | The new workspace's first fee, drawn from the moved credits. |
| `from` | `string \| null` | No | The personal org. After a move into a new workspace: the caller's new, empty personal org (the one before is `into`). |
| `into` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `keptApiKeys` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `machines` | `string[] \| null` | No | Sandbox IDs moved. |
| `madePaid` | `string[] \| null` | No | Moved free sandboxes that became paid. |
| `movedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `movedFunding` | `string \| null` | No | What pays the moved plan. |
| `movedTier` | `string \| null` | No | The personal plan moved into the workspace with its subscription, card and renewal date (a workspace without a plan of its own); nothing was charged for it, and endedTier is absent. unusedUsd is then the rest of this month's allowance, moved into the workspace's month. |
| `overageUsd` | `number \| null` | No | With movedTier: the personal usage so far beyond this month's allowance, billed at the move (credits first, then the card). |
| `paused` | `string[] \| null` | No | Running sandboxes paused for the move and resumed after it. |
| `returnedUsd` | `number \| null` | No | Of it, what went back into the grants that paid the fee (estimated on a dry run). |
| `secrets` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `snapshots` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `tier` | `string \| null` | No | The new workspace's plan. |
| `unusedUsd` | `number \| null` | No | Unused share of this month's personal fee, carried as workspace credit. |



---

### redeemInvite

`orgs.redeemInvite`

Join an org with an invite code. Signed-in sessions only

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.redeemInvite({ code: 'ABC123' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `code` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `allowedSpecs` | `object[] \| null` | No | The sizes that may be created in the org, the default first (its plan's, then any an admin granted the org). |
| `billing` | `object \| null` | No | The org's Stripe subscription (docs/pricing.md); absent when it never had one. |
| `cardMissingAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while a workspace has no card on file. On the free tier its sandboxes then answer `402 payment_method_required` and running ones are paused (`trigger: payment_method`); they run again once one is added (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/billing/checkout` with no tier, or the portal). |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `disabledAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while the workspace cannot be used: its plan ended (cancelled, unpaid, or its credits ran out). Its sandboxes answer `402 subscription_required`; they and its snapshots are deleted 30 days later unless it subscribes again. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `kind` | `string \| null` | No | `personal`: the account's own org, made when it signed up, on any tier but with no other members: it takes no invites. `workspace`: an org to share, on any tier; on the free tier it needs a card on file and has up to `limits.maxMembers` members. |
| `limits` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `personal` | `boolean \| null` | No | `kind` is `personal`. |
| `purgeAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set on a disabled workspace: when its sandboxes and snapshots are deleted. Until then its people can download their `/data` (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/exports`). |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | Your role in the org. |
| `tier` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `usage` | `object \| null` | No | The org's usage this calendar month (UTC) as the hourly rating job priced it: running time by sandbox size, the allocated disk (`/data`) of the paid sandboxes beyond the plan's included disk, and egress beyond the plan's free egress a month, up to `ratedUntil`. Snapshots and the system disk are not billed. |

### secrets.delete

Delete an org Secret and its explicit sandbox grants

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.secrets.delete({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd', name: 'OPENAI_API_KEY' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Organization ID. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | A unique environment variable name within an org. The names a sandbox's env reserves are reserved (shell variables such as PATH, HOME, BASH_ENV, ENV and IFS, session and trust-store variables, and INSTABOX_ and SSH_ prefixes). A Secret saved under a name before it was reserved is not delivered: the machine's guest status names it. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Instabox accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### secrets.import

`orgs.secrets.import`

Atomically create or replace a batch of org Secrets

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.secrets.import({
	id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd',
	values: {},
	delivery: 'proxy',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Organization ID. |
| `delivery` | `proxy \| env` | Yes | Proxy supplies an opaque sandbox-specific placeholder for substitution in ordinary HTTPS headers on allowed hosts (port 443). Env supplies the real value, including credentials needed by native database clients. Unsupported traffic never falls back from proxy to env. |
| `hosts` | `string[]` | No | Exact ASCII DNS hostnames, normalized to lowercase without a trailing dot, or a standalone `*` for every public HTTPS destination. Required for proxy delivery: omitted or empty is 400. No scheme, port, path, partial wildcard, IP literal, or local hostname. Destination checks also reject private and restricted resolved addresses. This scopes credential use, not all sandbox network access. |
| `values` | `Record<string, string>` | Yes | Names and values to replace atomically; omitted names stay unchanged. Null values are rejected. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">
