```ts
{
  createdAt?: string | null,
  delivery?: string | null,
  hosts?: string[] | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  orgId?: string | null,
  size?: number | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  version?: number | null
}[] | null
```

</AccordionGroup>

The names a sandbox's env reserves are reserved (shell variables such as PATH, HOME, BASH_ENV, ENV and IFS, session and trust-store variables, and INSTABOX_ and SSH_ prefixes). A Secret saved under a name before it was reserved is not delivered: the machine's guest status names it. |

Env supplies the real value, including credentials needed by native database clients. Unsupported traffic never falls back from proxy to env. |
| `hosts` | `string[]` | No | Exact ASCII DNS hostnames, normalized to lowercase without a trailing dot, or a standalone `*` for every public HTTPS destination. Required for proxy delivery: omitted or empty is 400. No scheme, port, path, partial wildcard, IP literal, or local hostname. Destination checks also reject private and restricted resolved addresses. This scopes credential use, not all sandbox network access. |

Env supplies the real value, including credentials needed by native database clients. Unsupported traffic never falls back from proxy to env. |

This scopes credential use, not all sandbox network access. |

### update

`orgs.update`

Rename an org. The owner and admins

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.update({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd', name: 'OPENAI_API_KEY' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Org ID (org_...). |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `allowedSpecs` | `object[] \| null` | No | The sizes that may be created in the org, the default first (its plan's, then any an admin granted the org). |
| `billing` | `object \| null` | No | The org's Stripe subscription (docs/pricing.md); absent when it never had one. |
| `cardMissingAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while a workspace has no card on file. On the free tier its sandboxes then answer `402 payment_method_required` and running ones are paused (`trigger: payment_method`); they run again once one is added (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/billing/checkout` with no tier, or the portal). |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `disabledAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set while the workspace cannot be used: its plan ended (cancelled, unpaid, or its credits ran out). Its sandboxes answer `402 subscription_required`; they and its snapshots are deleted 30 days later unless it subscribes again. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `kind` | `string \| null` | No | `personal`: the account's own org, made when it signed up, on any tier but with no other members: it takes no invites. `workspace`: an org to share, on any tier; on the free tier it needs a card on file and has up to `limits.maxMembers` members. |
| `limits` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `personal` | `boolean \| null` | No | `kind` is `personal`. |
| `purgeAt` | `string \| null` | No | Set on a disabled workspace: when its sandboxes and snapshots are deleted. Until then its people can download their `/data` (`POST /v1/orgs/{id}/exports`). |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | Your role in the org. |
| `tier` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `usage` | `object \| null` | No | The org's usage this calendar month (UTC) as the hourly rating job priced it: running time by sandbox size, the allocated disk (`/data`) of the paid sandboxes beyond the plan's included disk, and egress beyond the plan's free egress a month, up to `ratedUntil`. Snapshots and the system disk are not billed. |

### webhooks.deliveries.retry

`orgs.webhooks.deliveries.retry`

Send a finished (succeeded or failed) delivery once more

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.orgs.webhooks.deliveries.retry({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd', webhookId: 'wh_2r8m5x', deliveryId: 'dlv_6m3q8z' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Organization ID. The caller must be its owner or an admin. |
| `webhookId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `deliveryId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `attemptLog` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `eventId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `eventType` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `lastAttemptAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `lastError` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `lastStatus` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `nextAttemptAt` | `string \| null` | No | Pending only. |
| `payload` | `object \| null` | No | The body of every webhook request. Requests are signed per Standard Webhooks: `webhook-id` (the event ID: deduplicate on it), `webhook-timestamp` (Unix seconds) and `webhook-signature` (`v1,<base64 HMAC-SHA256 of "<id>.<timestamp>.<body>">`, space separated when two secrets sign). |
| `retryable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether POST .../retry would queue this delivery now: false while it is pending, and once its event is too old to retry (409 operation_conflict otherwise). |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Snapshots

### delete

`snapshots.delete`

Delete a snapshot

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.snapshots.delete({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Snapshot ID (`sn_`, or `im_` for older snapshots, + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |
| `idempotency_key` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `confirm_delete` | `string` | No | X-Confirm-Delete header: the ID or exact name of what is deleted. Left out, the plugin sends the id from the path. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Instabox accepted the call; it answered with no body. |



---

### files.list

`snapshots.files.list`

Read a file or directory of a snapshot's system files

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.snapshots.files.list({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Snapshot ID (`sn_`, or `im_` for older snapshots, + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |
| `path` | `string` | No | Absolute path, at most 4096 bytes (default "/"). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `binary` | `boolean \| null` | No | The file is not UTF-8 text; content is empty. |
| `content` | `string \| null` | No | A text file's content (UTF-8). |
| `entries` | `object[] \| null` | No | A directory's entries, directories first, then by name. |
| `modTime` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `mode` | `string \| null` | No | As ls -l, for example drwxr-xr-x. |
| `path` | `string \| null` | No | The path read, with symbolic links resolved. |
| `size` | `number \| null` | No | Bytes; 0 for a directory. |
| `truncated` | `boolean \| null` | No | The directory has more entries, or the file more bytes, than returned. |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### get

`snapshots.get`

Get a snapshot of one of your orgs

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.snapshots.get({ id: 'm_k3m7q2abx5wr4hj6zpcnfa2etd' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Snapshot ID (`sn_`, or `im_` for older snapshots, + 26 lowercase base32 characters). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `dataSizeBytes` | `number \| null` | No | The logical size of the storage disk (`/data`) a sandbox created from it gets, not the bytes stored (the saved data is sparse and compressed); absent for system-files-only snapshots. Sandboxes created from it get at least this much storage. |
| `defaultSpec` | `object \| null` | No | The size a sandbox created from this snapshot gets when the create names none: with `sizeLocked`, the vCPUs and memory of the sandbox it was saved from, the only size a create from it may name; without (saved before this rule), the default size (`limits.machineSpec`, 4 vCPU / 8 GiB on api.instabox.sh); a GPU snapshot: its GPU type's size for the count, which a create may change with `gpu`. Plus the storage (`/data`) and system disk sizes the snapshot was saved with, which a created sandbox gets at least. Absent when its template is unknown. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `error` | `string \| null` | No | Why a `failed` snapshot failed. |
| `exclusiveBytes` | `number \| null` | No | No longer computed; servers leave it out. (Was the stored bytes only this snapshot references.) |
| `guestUser` | `string \| null` | No | The source sandbox's Linux user (`Machine.guestUser`), which a sandbox created from this snapshot keeps unless the create names another. `dev` for snapshots of sandboxes from older templates. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | `sn_...` (`im_...` for older snapshots). |
| `model` | `string \| null` | No | The Mac model (`TemplateModel.name`) a macOS snapshot was saved on. A create from it runs there unless it names another `model` or that model is closed. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Unique among the org's live snapshots. |
| `orgId` | `string \| null` | No | The org the snapshot belongs to (its source sandbox's). |
| `publishedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the visibility became community or official. |
| `publisherLogin` | `string \| null` | No | Who shared or published it. |
| `rootSizeBytes` | `number \| null` | No | The logical size of the system disk a sandbox created from it gets (its provisioned capacity), not the bytes stored: a snapshot is saved sparse and compressed. |
| `sizeLocked` | `boolean \| null` | No | True for a snapshot (not of a GPU sandbox) saved since a create from a snapshot gets its source's size one to one: a create from it gets `defaultSpec`'s vCPUs and memory only (another is `400 spec_not_allowed`). Absent for older snapshots, which create at any size. |
| `sourceMachineId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sourceSnapshotSeq` | `number \| null` | No | The source sandbox's saved state (`storage.snapshotSeq`) this snapshot was taken from; 0 for a live snapshot of a sandbox that was never stopped. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | `ready` can be used. A snapshot of a running sandbox is `pending` while it uploads (its sizes are the sandbox's until then), then `ready` or `failed`. |
| `template` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `visibility` | `string \| null` | No | Who can create from the snapshot (docs/community-templates.md): `private` (the default, its org's members), `community` (every signed-in account) or `official` (listed next to the built-in templates). Absent on older servers: treat as private. Handlers do not set community or official yet. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="defaultSpec full type">

```ts
{
  gpuType?: string | null,
  gpus?: number | null,
  memMiB?: number | null,
  persistentDiskGiB?: number | null,
  rootDiskGiB?: number | null,
  vcpus?: number | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="template full type">

```ts
{
  digest?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  kernel?: string | null,
  version?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Usage

### get

`usage.get`

Your storage use, limits, this month's cost and credit balance

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.usage.get({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `org` | `string` | No | An org you belong to (org_...), personal or workspace, to report instead of the default. A workspace API key may name only its own workspace. An org you are not a member of, or that does not exist, answers `404 not_found`, as `GET /v1/orgs/{id}` does. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `allocatedDiskGiB` | `number \| null` | No | The `/data` of all your live sandboxes, in GiB, read live: what `limits.maxStoredGiB` is checked against and what storage is billed on. Absent from older servers. |
| `computedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the byte figures (`logicalBytes`, `storedBytes`, `storage`) were last computed. They are no longer updated: the scheduled measurement is off, so they are the last ones computed and may be stale, including nonzero for an org that has deleted everything since. An org never measured that stores nothing reads 0 as of now; null for an org with stored data that was never measured. Read `allocatedDiskGiB` instead. |
| `cost` | `object \| null` | No | This calendar month's (UTC) cost in USD as the hourly rating priced it (`costUSD`, with `runtimeUSD`, `storageUSD` and `egressUSD`), up to `ratedUntil`; the allowance (`allowanceUSD`, `creditRemainingUSD`, `overageUSD`) and the credit balance (`creditsUSD`, `creditsExpireAt`). The same figures as `usage` on `GET /v1/orgs/{id}`. A free sandbox is never billed, so a free month is `0`. Absent from older servers. |
| `exclusiveBytes` | `number \| null` | No | No longer computed: always 0. (Was the part of storedBytes no other org shares.) |
| `images` | `number \| null` | No | Deprecated: `snapshots` (same value). |
| `limits` | `object \| null` | No | Your limits. An admin may change maxMachines per user. |
| `logicalBytes` | `number \| null` | No | Full size of your sandboxes and snapshots, as of `computedAt` (no longer updated). |
| `machines` | `number \| null` | No | Your sandboxes, in any state. |
| `org` | `object \| null` | No | The org these figures and limits describe: your personal org by default, a workspace key's workspace, or the one named with `org`. Absent from older servers. |
| `snapshots` | `number \| null` | No | Your snapshots. |
| `storage` | `object \| null` | No | storedBytes by sandbox, from the same measurement (no longer updated). Absent for an org never measured, and from older servers. |
| `storedBytes` | `number \| null` | No | What they take up once compressed, with shared data counted once and the templates' own content (an unchanged root filesystem) not counted, as of `computedAt`. No longer updated: the limit and billing count `allocatedDiskGiB`. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="cost full type">

```ts

  allocatedDiskGiB?: number | null,
  allowanceUSD?: number | null,
  billableDiskGiB?: number | null,
  chargedUSD?: number | null,
  costUSD?: number | null,
```

```ts
{
  deletedMachineSnapshotBytes?: number | null,
  deletedMachineSnapshots?: number | null,
  machines?: {
    diskBytes?: number | null,
    id?: string | null,
    name?: string | null,
    sizeBytes?: number | null,
    snapshotBytes?: number | null,
    snapshots?: number | null,
    state?: string | null
  }[] | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### machines

`usage.machines`

Compute, disk and egress use and list price by sandbox over a window, deleted sandboxes included

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.usage.machines({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `org` | `string` | No | As in `GET /v1/usage`. |
| `q` | `string` | No | Only the sandboxes whose ID or name contains this, ignoring case. `total` counts the matches. |
| `metadata` | `string[]` | No | `key:value` (split at the first `:`); only sandboxes whose metadata has the key with exactly that value. Repeatable; all terms must match (AND). `total` counts the matches. |
| `from` | `string` | No | Start of the window (RFC 3339). Default the start of this month (UTC). |
| `to` | `string` | No | End of the window (RFC 3339), after `from`. Default now. |
| `sort` | `computeUSD \| vcpuSeconds \| memGiBSeconds` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | — |
| `offset` | `number` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `from` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `machines` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `to` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `total` | `number \| null` | No | Sandboxes in the list, all pages. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="machines full type">

```ts
{
  computeUSD?: number | null,
  deletedAt?: string | null,
  diskGiBSeconds?: number | null,
  diskUSD?: number | null,
  egressBytes?: number | null,
  egressUSD?: number | null,
  free?: boolean | null,
  gpuSeconds?: number | null,
  gpuType?: string | null,
  gpuUSD?: number | null,
  gpus?: number | null,
  id?: string | null,
  memGiBSeconds?: number | null,
  metadata?: Record<string, string> | null,
  name?: string | null | null,
  runningSeconds?: number | null,
  vcpuSeconds?: number | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Vault

### delete

`vault.delete`

Delete a Vault item. Sign-in sessions only

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.vault.delete({ name: 'OPENAI_API_KEY' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The item's name. An env item's name is the variable's name. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Instabox accepted the call; it answered with no body. |



---

### export

`vault.export`

Recover one retained legacy item as a private JSON download (needs a signed-in user token; an API key gets 403)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.vault.export({ name: 'OPENAI_API_KEY' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `item` | `object \| null` | No | Per-user Vault metadata (docs/vault.md). Values are absent from lists. |
| `value` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="item full type">

```ts
{
  createdAt?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  kind?: string | null,
  machines?: number | null,
  migrationReason?: string | null,
  migrationState?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  path?: string | null,
  selector?: Record<string, string> | null,
  size?: number | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### import

`vault.import`

Atomically import environment variables into the per-user Vault (needs a signed-in user token; an API key gets 403)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.vault.import({
	values: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `selector` | `Record<string, string>` | No | String labels. At most 32 keys of 1-64 characters `[A-Za-z0-9_.-]`, values of at most 512 characters, 8 KiB as JSON. |
| `values` | `Record<string, string>` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Instabox accepted the call; it answered with no body. |



---

### list

`vault.list`

The caller's Vault items, by name. Sign-in sessions only

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.vault.list({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  createdAt?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  kind?: string | null,
  machines?: number | null,
  migrationReason?: string | null,
  migrationState?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  path?: string | null,
  selector?: Record<string, string> | null,
  size?: number | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### set

`vault.set`

Create or replace a Vault item. Sign-in sessions only

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.vault.set({ name: 'OPENAI_API_KEY', kind: 'env', value: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The item's name. An env item's name is the variable's name. |
| `kind` | `env \| file` | Yes | — |
| `path` | `string` | No | File items only: the target, relative to the home directory. |
| `selector` | `Record<string, string>` | No | Absent or &#123;&#125;: the item applies to every machine. |
| `value` | `string` | Yes | UTF-8 text without NUL bytes, 1 byte to 64 KiB, stored as given. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | `vi_…` |
| `kind` | `string \| null` | No | The item's sandbox delivery kind. |
| `machines` | `number \| null` | No | The caller's live machines the item applies to (selector matches, Vault not off). |
| `migrationReason` | `string \| null` | No | Reason an item was retained for recovery. |
| `migrationState` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Unique per user. An env item's name is the variable's name. |
| `path` | `string \| null` | No | File items: the target, relative to the home directory. |
| `selector` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Historical labels; these do not control org Secret selection. |
| `size` | `number \| null` | No | The value's size in bytes. |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### update

`vault.update`

Change a Vault item's selector without resending its value. Sign-in sessions only

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.instabox.api.vault.update({
	name: 'OPENAI_API_KEY',
	selector: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The item's name. An env item's name is the variable's name. |
| `selector` | `Record<string, string>` | Yes | The new selector; &#123;&#125; clears it. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | `vi_…` |
| `kind` | `string \| null` | No | The item's sandbox delivery kind. |
| `machines` | `number \| null` | No | The caller's live machines the item applies to (selector matches, Vault not off). |
| `migrationReason` | `string \| null` | No | Reason an item was retained for recovery. |
| `migrationState` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Unique per user. An env item's name is the variable's name. |
| `path` | `string \| null` | No | File items: the target, relative to the home directory. |
| `selector` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Historical labels; these do not control org Secret selection. |
| `size` | `number \| null` | No | The value's size in bytes. |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
